Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，SED-struct始终实现最高的过滤精度，同时保持实用的过滤成本，尤其在异构树集合中表现优异。

Conclusion: SED-struct通过结合高效计算的SED下界与结构约束，解决了现有方法在剪枝精度与计算成本间的权衡问题，为复杂树数据的相似性搜索提供了更优解决方案。

Abstract: Threshold-based similarity search over tree-structured data using tree edit distance (TED) is computationally intensive. Given a query tree and a database of trees, the goal is to retrieve all trees within a predefined TED threshold $τ$. Because exact TED computation is expensive, practical methods employ lower-bounds to prune dissimilar candidates before verification. Existing lower-bounds exhibit a fundamental trade-off: inexpensive statistical and structural bounds provide limited pruning power, whereas the more precise traversal-based string edit distance (SED) bound is expensive to compute using standard quadratic dynamic programming. Moreover, previous comparative studies do not cover recent structural filters or threshold-aware SED implementations, leaving their practical trade-offs unclear. In this article, we first provide a comprehensive experimental comparison of state-of-the-art TED lower-bounds in terms of pruning precision and computational cost. We then accelerate the SED lower-bound using Ukkonen's bounded string edit distance algorithm, substantially reducing its runtime without affecting its pruning power. Finally, we introduce the SED-struct threshold filter, which strengthens SED with axes-aware constraints capturing structural relationships among tree nodes. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets show that SED-struct consistently achieves the highest filtering precision while retaining practical filtering costs. The results suggest that SED-struct is particularly beneficial for heterogeneous tree collections in which the standard SED lower-bound achieves relatively low precision.

</details>


### [179] [What Happens When the Model Eats the Stack? Rethinking the Research Agenda for Data Agents to Withstand the Bitter Lesson](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03141)
*Liana Patel,Siddharth Jha,Negar Arabzadeh,Carlos Guestrin,Ion Stoica,Matei Zaharia*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型（LLMs）快速内化传统数据代理能力所引发的“苦涩教训”，本文主张将研究重心从弥补模型局限转向构建“持久语义上下文”。未来数据系统的核心需求是原生支持这一抽象，通过高效的数据结构、存储、压缩及一致性协议，赋能处理复杂知识库的强大数据代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受“苦涩教训”启发，即端到端训练的LLM正迅速取代以往需精心 engineered 的数据代理功能。现有的系统层设计旨在补偿模型在特定任务上的局限性，但随着模型能力提升，这些补偿层将被模型本身吸收。因此，需要重新定位数据系统社区的研究方向。

Method: 基于实证洞察进行论证分析，提出以“持久语义上下文”（persistent semantic context）为核心的新范式。该范式强调为数据代理提供经过策展的环境上下文信息。同时，识别并阐述了实现该愿景所需的关键技术挑战，包括上下文数据结构、存储方法、压缩技术及语义一致性协议的设计。

Result: 研究发现，引入持久语义上下文层能显著提升数据代理的性能，但也带来了显著的系统级挑战。这确立了未来数据系统的一项关键要求：必须将持久语义上下文作为一等公民（first-class abstraction）进行原生服务，以支持能够处理庞大且复杂知识语料库的数据代理。

Conclusion: 未来的数据系统应围绕“持久语义上下文”进行重构，将其作为核心抽象层。通过设计高效的上下文管理技术（如存储、压缩和一致性协议），确保存储知识的完整性与正确性，从而释放数据代理在处理大规模复杂知识体系中的潜力。

Abstract: The bitter lesson poses an existential question for the data systems community, whereby large language models (LLMs) trained end-to-end are rapidly internalizing new capabilities that previously required carefully engineered data agents. Guided by empirical insights, we argue that as models continue to improve, many proposed system layers designed to compensate for model limitations on a given task will increasingly be subsumed by the model itself. We instead identify enduring research opportunities, which lie in supporting data agents across many queries with curated contextual information about the data environment, which we call persistent semantic context. We find that these context layers demonstrate strong promise for improving data agent performance, but they also raise significant system challenges. Thus, a key requirement for future data systems will lie in natively serving persistent semantic contexts as a first-class abstraction in order to enable capable data agents working over huge, complex knowledge corpora. Towards this vision, we outline exciting new research opportunities, including designing efficient context data structures, storage methods, compression techniques, and semantic consistency protocols, to ensure integrity and correctness of the stored contextual knowledge.

</details>


### [180] [Does SRL Pave the Road to Explainable Reasoning? Lessons Learned from an Implementer's Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03441)
*Lander Maes,Bryan-Elliott Tam,Jitse De Smet,Jos De Roo,Pieter Colpaert,Ruben Taelman*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文实现了两个Shape Rules Language (SRL)引擎（基于SPARQL的和专用的），验证了SRL规范在RDF推理中的可行性。结果表明，基于SPARQL的引擎开发成本低，而专用引擎速度更快（快2-6倍），证明SRL虽不成熟但已具备实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决RDF推理领域缺乏共享标准的问题。传统方法依赖固定的演绎体制（如RDFS、OWL）或非标准的规则语言，且SRL规范缺乏权威实现和构建指南，导致从业者难以建立合规引擎或评估其能力。

Method: 1. 开发了两个SRL推理引擎：一个复用现有SPARQL查询引擎及解析器，另一个为专用引擎。
2. 对两个引擎进行基准测试，评估其在经典RDF推理任务中的正确性（soundness）、完备性（completeness）和速度。
3. 使用SRL一致性测试套件及额外的用例驱动测试对引擎进行验证。

Result: 1. 基于SPARQL的引擎通过复用模块化解析器和SPARQL CONSTRUCT减少了开发工作量。
2. 专用引擎比SPARQL基础引擎快2到6倍，且随着规则集规模增大，性能差距进一步拉大。
3. 两个引擎均通过了SRL一致性测试套件。
4. 证明了可以在SPARQL引擎基础上低成本构建SRL引擎，虽然存在速度折衷，但可通过专用实现弥补。

Conclusion: 尽管SRL规范尚不成熟，但该语言已能支持实际有用的推理任务。开发者可以低成本地在SPARQL引擎之上构建可用的SRL引擎，若追求高性能则应选择专用实现。

Abstract: The Shape Rules Language (SRL) Working Draft defines how to derive new RDF triples from an RDF graph using inference rules. Each rule matches graph patterns and instantiates triple templates whose output feeds into validation pipelines, SPARQL queries, or further inference. RDF reasoning has traditionally relied on fixed entailment regimes (RDFS, OWL), rule-based ad-hoc languages such as N3, or other implementation-specific solutions without a shared standard. SRL introduces user-defined production rules with a defined grammar, dependency analysis, execution ordering, and termination guarantees. However, no authoritative implementation exists, leaving practitioners with little guidance on how to build a conformant engine or on what problems the language can solve. We implemented two SRL engines and evaluated both on classical RDF reasoning tasks for soundness, completeness, and speed. The first reuses an existing SPARQL query engine and its query parser; the second is a dedicated engine. The SPARQL-based engine reused an existing modular parser for query construction and SPARQL CONSTRUCT for triple production, reducing engine-specific work. The dedicated engine was two to six times faster, the gap widening as rule sets grow. Both engines were validated against the SRL conformance test suite, supplemented by additional use-case-driven tests. A usable SRL engine can be built inexpensively on top of a SPARQL engine, with a moderate speed trade-off that a dedicated implementation recovers. Despite the specification's immaturity, the language already supports practically useful reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [181] [KnowFeat: Knowledge-Guided Feature Engineering via LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03529)
*Chengsong You,Wangyue Li,Weiqiao Que,Qizhou Chen,Kunyan Wu,Wei Deng,Feng Zhu,Xiaofeng He*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出KnowFeat框架，利用LLM结合结构化领域知识进行自动化特征工程，通过三阶段验证管道确保特征质量与可追溯性，在多个基准测试中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型（LLM）的表格数据自动特征工程方法缺乏结构化领域知识、严格的验证机制以及可解释的来源追溯能力。

Method: 构建KnowFeat框架，将领域知识组织为五种类型（模式元数据、监管指标、检测规则、专家意见、法庭文档证据）并注入LLM代理；实施三阶段验证管道（代码执行、统计质量检查、模型有效性评估）；为每个被接受的特征生成来源记录以追踪其设计依据。

Result: 在严格排除特征选择泄漏的协议下，KnowFeat在12个公共基准测试中排名第一（平均排名2.3），优于其他7种方法（单侧Wilcoxon p=0.017），在电信流失数据集上AUC提升最高达11.6个百分点；在真实比特币反洗钱（Elliptic）和合成数字货币反洗钱（SimECNY）数据集上也保持了具有竞争力的检测性能及完整的来源可追溯性。

Conclusion: KnowFeat通过整合结构化领域知识和严格的验证流程，显著提升了特征工程的质量、性能及可解释性，证明了其在复杂金融场景下的有效性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Automated feature engineering with large language models (LLMs) can produce semantically meaningful features for tabular data, yet existing methods lack structured domain knowledge, rigorous verification, and explainable provenance. We propose KnowFeat, a knowledge-guided feature engineering framework that organizes domain knowledge into five types -- schema metadata, regulatory indicators, detection rules, expert opinions, and court document evidence -- and injects them as structured context into an LLM agent. A three-stage verification pipeline filters candidates through code execution, statistical quality checks, and model effectiveness evaluation. Every accepted feature carries a provenance record tracing its design to specific knowledge assets. Under a strict held-out protocol that eliminates feature-selection leakage, KnowFeat ranks first (avg. rank 2.3) across twelve public benchmarks among seven methods (one-sided Wilcoxon p=0.017), with a peak gain of +11.6 pp AUC on a telecom churn dataset. On a real-world Bitcoin anti-money laundering (AML) dataset (Elliptic) and a synthetic digital currency AML benchmark (SimECNY), KnowFeat maintains competitive detection performance with full provenance traceability.

</details>


### [182] [Tree Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03538)
*Nicolas Spyratos*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出一种基于标签有向树的新型数据库模型，通过统一的函数代数框架整合遍历查询与分析查询，支持继承、关系型数据兼容及友好界面访问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统关系模型中分析查询（如SQL Group-by）与关系代数分离的问题，旨在实现数据访问与数据分析在同一形式化框架内的统一。

Method: 构建以根节点为实体、非根节点为属性的标签有向树结构；定义一套树操作和基于简单函数代数（限制、组合、配对、笛卡尔积）的查询语言，包含遍历查询和分析查询两类。

Result: 证明该模型具有强大的表达能力：无缝支持继承机制；可在树数据库上层定义一致的关系型数据库；提供用户友好的接口以访问和分析关系型数据。

Conclusion: 该树数据库模型通过将遍历查询作为分析查询的基础块，实现了数据访问与分析的统一框架，优于传统关系模型的处理方式。

Abstract: We propose a novel database model whose basic structure is a labeled, directed tree with node identities. Intuitively, the root of the tree is seen as an object (or entity), the non-root nodes as attributes of the object and the semantics of each attribute is represented by the unique path leading from the root to the attribute. We define a tree database to be a set of such trees. The trees of the database can be combined to produce new trees using a set of operations on trees that we define in the paper. The query language of our model offers two types of queries, traversal queries and analytic queries. A query (whether traversal or analytic) is always defined over a tree, which is either a tree in the database or a tree derived from other trees using tree operations. The operations on trees and the query language are both defined using a simple functional algebra whose operations are: restriction of a function, composition of functions, pairing of functions and Cartesian product of sets.
  A distinctive feature of our model is that traversal queries and analytic queries are both defined within the same formal framework; and in fact, traversal queries serve as the building blocks for analytic queries. This is in sharp contrast to the relational model, where analytic queries are defined outside the relational algebra, in the form of SQL Group-by queries. Therefore our model supports data access and data analysis within the same formal framework.
  We demonstrate the expressive power of our model by showing: (a) how our model can support inheritance in a seamless manner, (b) how one can define consistent relational databases on top of a tree database - with the tree database playing the role of an underlying semantic layer and (c) how a tree database can be used as a user-friendly interface for accessing and analyzing relational data.

</details>


### [183] [Convolution Sum-Product Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03672)
*Kyle Deeds,Timo Camillo Merkl,Dan Suciu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出卷积和积查询（CSPQs），允许原子包含变量的线性组合。通过引入秩分析、商空间和变量替换等线性代数技术，改进了最坏情况最优连接和树分解算法，实现了更高效的查询评估，并将方法扩展至任意域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的处理线性不等式约束的技术在评估CSPQs时渐近表现次优。CSPQs在图像处理等实际场景及$(\min,+)$-卷积等理论研究中具有重要应用，需要更高效的评估算法。

Method: 1. 将最坏情况最优连接（WCOJ）适配到CSPQs；2. 扩展树分解（TD）定义以支持线性组合作为一等公民，并描述因子化执行；3. 定义三种宽度度量以限制复杂度；4. 证明方法可应用于有理数以外的任意域。

Result: 提出了针对CSPQs的多种评估算法，其运行时间与合取查询类似；通过扩展树分解实现了因子化执行，并利用定义的宽度度量控制了相对于域值大小、活动域大小和关系支撑集大小的复杂度。

Conclusion: 研究提出的基于线性代数的算法克服了先前技术的次优性，为包含变量线性组合的查询提供了高效且通用的评估框架，适用于更广泛的数学结构。

Abstract: We study query evaluation for an extension of sum-product queries (SPQ) that allows atoms with linear combinations of variables (e.g. $A(2X+3Y-Z, Y+Z)$), which we call convolution sum-product queries (CSPQs). These queries arise in both practical settings (e.g. image processing workloads) and theoretical ones (e.g. the $(\min,+)$-convolution and $k$-SUM conjectures), and capture SPQs with added linear equality constraints. While prior work has considered the impact of linear in- and dis-equality constraints on query evaluation, the techniques developed in that setting are asymptotically sub-optimal for CSPQs. To address this, we describe several evaluation algorithms for CSPQs that leverage linear algebra techniques like rank analysis, quotient spaces, and variable substitution. First, we adapt Worst-Case Optimal Joins to CSPQs, achieving a runtime similar in spirit to that for conjunctive queries. Then, we extend the definition of tree decompositions (TDs) to CSPQs, and describe a factorized execution. In this extension, linear combinations are first-class citizens and play the same role as variables in traditional TDs. We define three width measures that bound the complexity of this execution with respect to the size of the domain values, of the active domain, and of the relation's support. Lastly, we show how these methods can be applied to arbitrary fields beyond the rationals.

</details>


### [184] [Aker: Density-Aware Approximate Caching for Vector Search (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03712)
*Sukjoon Oh,Minki Kang,Dohyun Kim,Baotong Lu,Jing Liu,Qianxi Zhang,Qi Chen,Youjip Won*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Aker 是一种针对基于磁盘的近似最近邻搜索（ANNS）的近似缓存系统。它通过动态调整相似度阈值来适应局部邻居密度，并采用“删除即时、插入延迟”的一致性模型解决缓存更新问题，显著提升了查询准确率（最高+64%）和吞吐量（最高3.2倍），同时降低内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于磁盘的 ANNS 因频繁磁盘访问导致高 I/O 开销。虽然近似缓存可复用历史查询结果以绕过昂贵搜索，但现有方法存在两大局限：1) 近似命中谓词无法兼顾高吞吐与高精度，未能适应高维空间中变化的局部邻居密度；2) 缺乏有效的刷新机制，在向量更新时难以维持缓存正确性。

Method: 提出 Aker 系统，包含两个核心设计：1) 引入每查询相似度阈值机制，每个缓存条目维护独立且动态调整的阈值，根据观察到的缓存命中模式自适应局部邻居密度；2) 提出 del-consistency 一致性模型，对删除操作执行即时处理（eagerly），对插入操作执行延迟处理（lazily），实现低开销刷新机制以限制缓存陈旧度。将 Aker 集成至 pgvector 进行评估。

Result: 在代表性工作负载下，Aker 相比先前方案召回率最高提升 64 个百分点，QPS 最高提升 3.2 倍，且仅使用 pgvector 共享缓冲区 0.6 倍的内存空间。

Conclusion: Aker 通过自适应阈值和新型一致性模型，有效解决了磁盘 ANNS 中缓存效率、精度与更新一致性的平衡问题，显著优化了性能与资源利用率。

Abstract: Disk-based approximate nearest neighbor search (ANNS) incurs high I/O overhead due to frequent disk accesses during index traversal. Approximate caching, which reuses the results of past queries to serve future similar queries, offers a promising approach to bypass expensive disk searches. However, existing approaches suffer from two key limitations. First, their approximate hit predicates fail to simultaneously achieve high throughput and high accuracy, as they do not adapt to the varying local neighbor density in high-dimensional spaces. Second, they lack an effective refresh mechanism to maintain cache correctness under vector updates.
  We present Aker, an approximate cache for disk-based ANNS. Aker addresses these limitations through two core design choices. First, we introduce a per-query similarity threshold, where each cache entry maintains its own threshold that is dynamically adjusted based on observed cache hit patterns. This design enables Aker to adapt to neighborhood densities to preserve both efficiency and accuracy. Second, we propose del-consistency, a consistency model for ANNS caches that applies deletions eagerly and insertions lazily. Under this model, Aker implements a low-overhead refresh mechanism that bounds cache staleness and preserves high search accuracy. We integrate Aker into pgvector and evaluate it on representative workloads. Aker improves recall by up to 64 percentage points over prior solutions and increases QPS by up to 3.2x, while using 0.6x the memory of pgvector's shared buffers.

</details>


### [185] [From Data Querying to Data Investigations: Rethinking Natural Language Interfaces for Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03898)
*Fabian Wenz,Zixuan Chen,Carsten Binnig*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出“数据调查”新范式，突破传统Text-to-SQL单次查询限制，通过D^2系统实现多步SQL推理与证据支持的数据问题解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Text-to-SQL范式假设用户问题可由单次SQL查询回答，但实际用户需通过序列SQL查询及中间结果推理来解决复杂数据问题。

Method: 引入“数据调查”范式，开发D^2原型系统，自主搜索、推理并收集数据；基于Murder Mystery数据集构建新基准进行评估。

Result: D^2在需要数据调查和证据支持决策的任务中展现出潜力，超越了传统单查询问答的能力。

Conclusion: 数据调查范式及D^2系统为NL数据库接口提供了更强大的解决方案，适用于复杂数据问题分析场景。

Abstract: Natural language (NL) interfaces to databases have been optimized for the wrong problem. The dominant Text-to-SQL paradigm assumes that users ask questions that can be answered by single SQL queries. In practice, however, users seek assistance with solving data problems. This requires searching a database by sequences of SQL queries while reasoning over intermediate results instead of just running one SQL query. This paper therefore introduces a new paradigm for NL interfaces to data, which we call data investigations. We present D^2, a first prototype of a data investigation system that embodies this vision by autonomously searching, reasoning over, and collecting data to solve data problems. Using a newly constructed benchmark based on the Murder Mystery dataset, we demonstrate the potential of D^2 for tasks that require data investigations with evidence-backed decisions, extending beyond the capabilities of traditional single-query question answering.

</details>


### [186] [Property Testing for Recursive Query Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03908)
*Isolde Adler,Carsten Lutz,Quentin Manière,Marcin Przybyłko,Lukas Schulze*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文研究递归查询语言（2RPQ和Monadic Datalog）的属性测试问题，发现非2RPQ答案可常数时间测试，而受限线性递归的Monadic Datalog也可测；但若Monadic Datalog不等价于α-无环程序且规则体自连接自由，则不可常数时间测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Chen和Yoshida (2019) 关于布尔合取查询（CQ）属性测试的结果，即非满足性可测试当且仅当查询等价于α-无环查询，旨在将这一理论框架扩展至更复杂的递归查询语言领域。

Method: 分析双向正则路径查询（2RPQs）和Monadic Datalog程序的属性测试复杂性，通过构造算法证明可测试性，并通过归约或反证法证明不可测试性，特别关注α-无环性、线性递归及自连接自由等约束条件。

Result: 1. 任何2RPQ的非答案均可使用常数次数量的完成查询和大小查询以单侧误差进行测试。
2. 对于递归限制为线性且规则体为α-无环的Monadic Datalog程序子集，同样具备常数查询可测试性。
3. 假设所有规则体自连接自由，若Monadic Datalog程序Π不等价于α-无环程序，则其假值不可进行常数查询的单侧误差测试。
4. 存在某些轻度非线性但规则体为α-无环的程序仍具备常数查询可测试性。

Conclusion: 研究确立了2RPQ在属性测试中的良好行为，并明确了Monadic Datalog中α-无环性与递归结构对可测试性的关键影响，揭示了从非递归到递归查询过渡时的计算复杂性界限，同时指出了部分开放问题。

Abstract: In the context of database querying, property testing provides a framework for testing query answers with high confidence while inspecting only a sublinear part of the database, through completion queries and size queries. A fundamental result of Chen and Yoshida (2019) states that non-satisfaction of a Boolean conjunctive query $q$ is testable with a constant number of such queries and one-sided error if and only if $q$ is equivalent to an $α$-acyclic query. In this article, we initiate the study of property testing for recursive query languages, focusing on two-way regular path queries (2RPQs) and monadic Datalog. One of our main results is positive: non-answers to any 2RPQ are constant query testable with one-sided error. We extend this slightly to a certain class of monadic Datalog programs in which recursion is restricted to be linear and rule bodies must be $α$-acyclic. Turning towards unrestricted monadic Datalog, we next show that if a monadic Datalog program $Π$ is not equivalent to an $α$-acyclic program, then falsity of $Π$ is not constant query testable with one-sided error. This is under the assumption that all rule-bodies are self-join free. We leave open the case of monadic Datalog programs with $α$-acyclic rule bodies that are not restricted to linear recursion, but observe as a first step that there exist $α$-acyclic programs that are mildly non-linear and constant query testable with one-sided error.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [187] [CHSR-RRF: A curriculum-gated hybrid retrieval framework with reciprocal rank fusion and leakage-aware benchmarking for educational RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02913)
*Terence Ateya,Zavier Ndum Ndum,Jicheng Fu,Kelly Tendongkeng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对教育问答中RAG存在的“课程泄露”问题，提出CHSR-RRF框架和CERB基准。研究证明在检索前通过元数据约束过滤候选集，比检索后排序更能有效防止内容越界，同时保持召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准检索器仅优化主题相关性，导致在教育场景中可能出现虽相关但不符合学科、年级或考试语境的内容（即“课程泄露”），影响教学有效性。

Method: 提出CHSR-RRF框架：先应用元数据约束进行预检索门控，再结合稀疏与稠密搜索及 reciprocal rank fusion 进行确定性重排序；构建CERB基准测试集以评估课程约束下的检索性能。

Result: 预检索门控使课程泄露减少4.6倍且保持排名召回率；若仅在检索后应用约束，则召回率和精确范围成功率降至零。分析显示部分剩余失败源于语料库和元数据缺口。

Conclusion: 结构化教育领域的检索应被视为受限选择任务，必须在候选集形成阶段（而非排序后）强制执行有效性约束。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is increasingly used in educational question answering, but standard retrievers optimize topical relevance without enforcing curriculum validity. In school settings, a passage can be relevant yet inappropriate if it comes from the wrong subject, level, or examination context; we call this failure mode curriculum leakage. We present CHSR-RRF, a curriculum-gated hybrid retrieval framework that applies metadata constraints before retrieval, then combines sparse and dense search with reciprocal rank fusion and deterministic reranking. We also introduce CERB, a 126-case benchmark for curriculum-constrained retrieval with hierarchy-aware relevance labels and explicit leakage annotations. On a 61-case pilot, pre-retrieval gating reduces leakage by 4.6x ($p<0.001$) while preserving ranked recall, whereas applying the same constraints after retrieval collapses recall and exact-scope success to zero ($p=0.039$). A full-benchmark lower-bound analysis further shows that many remaining failures arise from corpus and metadata gaps rather than retrieval design alone. These results show that retrieval in structured educational domains should be treated as constrained selection, with validity enforced when the candidate pool is formed rather than after ranking.

</details>


### [188] [Reflect-SQL: A Self-Reflection Based Framework for Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02944)
*Anupreksha Jain,Manish Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出Reflect-SQL框架，通过多阶段自反思机制解决Text-to-SQL在复杂数据库中的架构理解、检索和SQL生成问题，在BIRD基准上达到72.03%的执行准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Text-to-SQL在实际应用中面临的三大挑战：模糊且庞大的数据库架构、因结构化设置和模糊查询导致的表/列检索失效、以及缺乏鲁棒验证机制导致的SQL语法或逻辑错误。

Method: 引入基于多阶段自反思的Reflect-SQL框架：1. 利用知识库理解模糊架构；2. 建立有效检索流程；3. 使用LLM-as-a-judge评分机制和互联反馈循环（包括反馈驱动检索循环、综合验证循环、蕴含优化循环）迭代 refine 结果。

Result: 在具有挑战性的BIRD基准测试中，执行准确率达到72.03%，显著优于当前最先进（SOTA）基线模型。

Conclusion: Reflect-SQL通过整合多层反思机制，弥合了用户意图与复杂数据之间的差距，展示了在企业应用中对可靠性的大幅提升。

Abstract: Democratizing data access through natural language is a crucial goal for modern enterprises, but the practical adoption of Text-to-SQL is critically hindered by real-world complexities: 1. Obscure and large database schemas, 2. Ineffective retrieval of relevant tables and columns due to structured setting of schemas and vague user query, 3. Generation of syntactically or logically flawed SQL due to a lack of robust validation and correction mechanism. To address these systemic challenges, we introduce Reflect-SQL, a novel framework for Text to SQL, grounded in multi-stage self-reflection approach to develop understanding of obscure schema using a knowledge base, setup a process for effective retrieval and system to generate syntactically/semantically SQL. Instead of a single-pass attempt, our system employs an LLM-as-a-judge driven scoring mechanism within interconnected feedback loops to iteratively refine the results at every stage. A feedback-driven retrieval loop refines the user's natural language query, while a synthesis loop validates and corrects the SQL and finally, an entailment loop optimizes the end-to-end process and continuously enriches the knowledge base. By integrating these layers of reflection, Reflect-SQL bridges the critical gap between user intent and complex data. On the challenging BIRD benchmark, our framework achieves an execution accuracy of 72.03%, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art baselines, demonstrating a major leap in reliability for enterprise applications.

</details>


### [189] [UniCon: A Unified Context-Centric Modeling Paradigm for CTR Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03290)
*Jiajun Cui,Zhengqi Xu,Fan Zhang,Zhangteng,Gu Tang,Honghong Zhu,Mengxi Wu,Yulin Liang,Xingxing Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出UniCon架构，将用户历史行为与当前请求统一为同质上下文单元进行建模，通过局部耦合和动态演化注意力机制提升CTR预测效果，在美团搜索广告中显著提升了RPM、CTR和收入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一建模方法在Token层面区分序列与非序列信号，这种基于传统特征工程的划分与用户决策过程不符，掩盖了结构依赖关系，限制了扩展效率和预测质量，尤其在电商货架等丰富上下文场景中问题突出。

Method: 提出UniCon统一上下文中心建模架构：1) 将请求上下文作为基本建模单元，历史行为和预测目标视为同质上下文单元；2) 使用Intra-context attention捕捉上下文内物品间的局部耦合（Locality）；3) 使用Inter-context attention建模跨上下文的决策状态动态演化（Dynamics）；4) 采用上下文单元级序列压缩降低部署开销。

Result: 在美团搜索广告场景下，离线AUC比强基线提升0.0139；线上实现统计显著的增益：RPM提升3.09%，CTR提升2.07%，收入提升2.95%。

Conclusion: UniCon通过弥合历史与目标之间的结构差距，支持更有效的统一CTR模型扩展，证明了以上下文为中心的同质化建模优于传统的异构信号分割方法。

Abstract: Unified modeling has become a major direction for industrial click-through rate (CTR) prediction. Existing approaches typically unify sequential and non-sequential signals at the token level, model their interactions in a shared backbone, and increase model capacity to improve scaling behavior. However, this division originates from legacy feature-engineering practice and is misaligned with the underlying decision process. User behavior is inherently a sequence of homogeneous context units; at the level of input organization, historical behavior and the current request differ only in whether their outcomes are observed or remain to be predicted. Treating them as heterogeneous signals obscures structural dependencies within the user's decision context, limiting both scaling efficiency and prediction quality. This limitation is particularly pronounced in context-rich scenarios such as e-commerce shelves and waterfall feeds. To address this, we propose UniCon, a unified context-centric modeling architecture that treats the request context as the basic modeling unit and organizes history and prediction targets as homogeneous context units. Intra-context attention captures local coupling among items within a context (Locality), while inter-context attention models the dynamic evolution of decision states across contexts (Dynamics). This organization bridges the structural gap between history and target and supports more effective scaling of unified CTR models. Context-unit-level sequence compression further reduces deployment overhead. On Meituan search advertising, UniCon improves offline AUC by 0.0139 over a strong production baseline and achieves statistically significant online lifts of 3.09% in RPM, 2.07% in CTR, and 2.95% in revenue.

</details>


### [190] [DoPR: Reusable Compressed Document Prefixes for Efficient LLM Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03311)
*Beiya Dai,Yifan Wei,Guang Yang,Xing Shi,Xinbing Wang,Zhouhan Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出DoPR框架，通过解耦离线文档处理与在线重排序，利用压缩的文档前缀状态实现高效的查询-文档对评分，显著降低计算成本并保留高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大型语言模型（LLMs）在点式重排序中因重复处理同一文档而导致的巨大冗余计算问题。

Method: 构建压缩文档前缀框架（DoPR），预先选择查询无关的文档表示并将其转换为压缩的前缀状态进行离线预计算；在线重排序时仅处理查询和评分token，文档信息直接调用存储的前缀状态。

Result: 在TREC DL、BEIR和BRIGHT数据集上，使用Qwen3系列模型验证，实现了高达8.0倍的在线文档侧内存减少和8.04倍的速度提升，同时保持了匹配的全文档重排器97.1%-99.5%的平均NDCG@10准确率。

Conclusion: DoPR通过文档侧压缩和前缀状态复用，有效降低了在线重排序的计算开销，证明了该架构在保持高性能的同时具备显著的效率优势。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are effective rerankers, but pointwise reranking repeatedly processes the same document across different queries, causing substantial redundant document-side computation. We propose \textbf{DoPR}, a compressed document prefix framework that decouples offline document processing from online reranking. DoPR first selects query-independent document representations and converts them into compressed document prefix states, which are precomputed offline and reused whenever the document is retrieved. During online reranking, the model scores each query-document pair by processing only the query and scoring token, with document information supplied by the stored prefix states. This design reduces online cost through both document-side compression and cross-query prefix-state reuse. Experiments on TREC DL, BEIR, and BRIGHT with Qwen3 models from $0.6$B to $8$B show that DoPR achieves up to 8.0$\times$ online document-side memory reduction and up to 8.04$\times$ latency speedup, while retaining \textbf{97.1\%-99.5\%} of the average NDCG@10 of matched full-document rerankers.

</details>


### [191] [SelfDR: Self-Distillation from Reasoning for LLM-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03313)
*Chumeng Jiang,Jiayin Wang,Xinjie Lin,Zhiqiang Guo,Hengliang Luo,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出SelfDR框架，通过自蒸馏技术将大语言模型（LLM）的推理能力直接转化为高效推荐，解决显式生成推理轨迹带来的计算成本问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然推理能提升LLM在推荐系统中的准确性，但显式生成中间推理痕迹导致高昂的计算成本，限制了其在现实推荐系统中的实际部署。

Method: 提出SelfDR（基于推理的自蒸馏框架）。构建教师推荐器：训练一个Reasoner以后端性能为奖励，生成针对性理由并融入输入；构建学生推荐器：使用相同基座LLM，通过动态加权策略从教师处进行自蒸馏，实现直接推荐。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上的实验验证了SelfDR在推荐有效性、合理性及推理效率方面的优势。

Conclusion: SelfDR成功实现了在不依赖外部模型的情况下，兼顾推荐效果与推理效率，为LLM-based推荐提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently emerged as powerful backbones for recommendation. To better elicit their capabilities, reasoning has been widely incorporated to help LLMs interpret rich textual signals and improve recommendation accuracy. However, explicitly generating intermediate reasoning traces often incurs substantial computational costs, which limits practical deployment in real-world recommender systems. To address this challenge, we propose SelfDR, a Self-Distillation from Reasoning framework for LLM-based Recommendation. SelfDR distills an LLM's own reasoning-enhanced predictions to produce recommendations directly, improving recommendation effectiveness while maintaining inference efficiency. All components in the framework are built on the same base LLM, without relying on any external models. Specifically, the teacher recommender is constructed by training a reasoner with downstream performance as the reward, enabling it to generate targeted rationales that are later incorporated into the teacher's input. A student recommender for direct recommendation, with the same underlying model, then learns from the teacher through self-distillation with a dynamic weighting strategy. Extensive experiments on three public datasets validate the effectiveness, rationality, and efficiency of SelfDR. Codes are available at https://github.com/JiangDeccc/SelfDistillation.

</details>


### [192] [SciLENS: RL-Driven Autonomous Agents for Scientific Localized Evidence Navigation and Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03338)
*Leqi Zheng,Jinbo Su,Yuying Li,Chaokun Wang,Weiping Wang,Haitao Li,Jiajun Zhang,Shannan Yan,Zhaolu Kang,Rong Fu,Jie Wu,Fang Niu,Hang Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SciLENS是一个完全本地化的自主科学文献合成智能体框架，通过整合结构可视化技术优化推理过程，并在多项基准测试中达到与GPT-5.2和Gemini-3.0-pro相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有科学文献合成智能体过度依赖专有在线服务所导致的可复现性差、隐私泄露风险高以及无法离线部署的问题。

Method: 1. 构建基于双层级基础设施的本地化框架，索引约1200万学术记录；2. 将结构可视化作为行动工具集成至推理循环中，以压缩复杂的引用拓扑并缓解上下文耗尽；3. 开发自动化数据合成管道，从引用知识图谱中提取多跳子图，并通过20个前沿模型进行交叉一致性验证；4. 采用逆向分解规范策略进行对齐，为早期规划和严格证据 grounding 提供细粒度过程奖励。

Result: 在涵盖标准问答、引用准确性、事实推理和结构合成的六个科学基准测试中，SciLENS显著优于开源基线模型，性能表现可与GPT-5.2和Gemini-3.0-pro相媲美。

Conclusion: SciLENS成功实现了无需人工标注训练的高效本地化科学文献合成，证明了结构可视化和自动化数据合成在提升智能体推理能力和降低对专有API依赖方面的有效性。

Abstract: Scientific literature synthesis agents increasingly rely on proprietary online services, limiting reproducibility, privacy, and offline deployment. To address this challenge, we introduce SciLENS Scientific Localized Evidence Navigation and Synthesis), a fully local autonomous agent framework operating on a dual-tier infrastructure indexing approximately 12 million academic records. SciLENS pioneers the integration of structural visualization as an actionable tool within the reasoning loop, enabling the agent to compress complex citation topologies into validated data-driven charts and thereby mitigate context exhaustion during macro-level synthesis. To train the agent without human annotation, we develop an automated data synthesis pipeline that extracts multi-hop subgraphs from a citation knowledge graph, verified by cross-model consensus among 20 frontier models. The agent is subsequently aligned through a reverse-decomposition rubric strategy that provides fine-grained process rewards for early planning and strict evidence grounding. Evaluations across six scientific benchmarks encompassing standard QA, citation accuracy, factual reasoning, and structural synthesis demonstrate that SciLENS significantly outperforms open-source baselines and achieves performance comparable to GPT-5.2 and Gemini-3.0-pro. Our source code and data are released at https://github.com/LQgdwind/SciLENS.

</details>


### [193] [HypRQ-VAE: Hyperbolic Item Indexing for Long-Tail-Aware Generative Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03369)
*Longfeng Wu,Tong Zeng,Giovanni Seni,Zhimin Peng,Bhanu Pratap Singh Rawat,Si Zhang,Yao Zhou,Lecheng Zheng,Bo Ji,Yujun Yan,Dawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出HypRQ-VAE框架，利用双曲空间学习项目索引，解决生成式推荐中LLM文本令牌与离散项目索引的错位及长尾分布建模难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐方法将推荐视为语言建模任务，但LLM处理文本令牌而推荐系统依赖离散项目索引，导致幻觉问题；且现有欧几里得空间方法难以有效模拟真实目录中长尾分布（头部项目主导，尾部项目反映小众偏好）。

Method: 提出双曲残差量化变分自编码器（HypRQ-VAE），首个在双曲空间中学习项目索引的框架。利用双曲几何指数体积扩张特性，自然适配用户-物品交互的幂律结构，编码丰富文本语义同时保留稀疏长尾项目的表示保真度。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的实验表明，HypRQ-VAE显著提升了推荐性能，特别是在推荐尾部项目方面表现优异。

Conclusion: 双曲空间在建模生成式推荐中的项目层次结构和稀疏性方面具有优越能力，从而带来性能增益。

Abstract: Sequential recommender systems model user behavior as item ID sequences, while recent generative methods cast recommendation as a language modeling task using large language models (LLMs). While this paradigm incorporates rich textual semantics, it introduces a fundamental mismatch: LLMs operate on text tokens, whereas recommender systems depend on discrete item indices. This misalignment often leads to hallucinations in generative recommendations. Existing methods attempt to bridge this gap by learning item vocabularies in Euclidean space, but they struggle to model the inherent long-tail distribution of real-world catalogs, where a small number of head items dominate, and a vast number of tail items reflect users' niche preferences. To address this issue, we introduce Hyperbolic Residual-Quantized Variational AutoEncoder (HypRQ-VAE), the first framework to learn item indexing in hyperbolic space. HypRQ-VAE leverages the unique properties of hyperbolic geometry, whose exponential volume expansion naturally accommodates the power law structure of user-item interactions. This allows the model to encode rich textual semantics while preserving the representational fidelity of sparse, long-tail items. Experiments on three benchmark datasets show that HypRQ-VAE significantly improves the performance of recommendation, particularly in recommending tail items. Our analysis attributes these gains to the superior capacity of hyperbolic space to model item hierarchies and sparsity in generative recommendation. Our code and data are available at: https://github.com/wulongfeng/HypRQ-VAE.

</details>


### [194] [Plan Pointers and Record-Directive Form in Budgeted Verification of Inherited Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03450)
*Kazuki Nakayashiki*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究通过12项注册实验（共14,760次尝试），分析了不同形式的指令（指针、标准或两者结合）如何影响具有短记忆限制的智能体选择存档记录的行为。结果显示，特定模型对指令格式的响应存在显著差异：部分模型中长度匹配的标准优于单纯ID，但在某些Claude模型上追加ID会抵消标准的效果；尽管存在交互效应，但通过增加“认证行”和预算可恢复目标选择。结论指出这些结果仅为对固定面板上精确编辑的描述性效应，不声称机制解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在智能体仅继承少量短期记忆（六条一行记忆）且需从存档源中选择记录的约束下，不同的指令写入形式（如指向记录的指针、识别标准或两者组合）如何具体引导和改变其决策行为。

Method: 在一条仪器谱系的12项注册研究中，对14,760次尝试进行测量。涉及六种直接提供程序模型及OpenRouter服务面板。实验设计包括对比不同指令格式（ID vs 标准）、观察追加ID对标准的抵消效应、测试字节级编辑的影响、以及在不同存储环境和重复运行（每单元格80次）下的复现情况。使用统计区间评估效果显著性。

Result: 1. 在某些模型（Study D）中，长度匹配的标准比单纯ID高出35.0分；但在OpenRouter面板（Study E）上未通过优越性规则。
2. 在三个Claude模型上，追加ID完全抵消了标准的效果（Study F-x）。
3. 六个字节匹配的编辑显示每个字符串有独立效果（Study G）。
4. 高置信度重复运行显示大部分对比结果在边际内或未决（Study G'）。
5. 添加认证行（+96.0分）和两信用点预算使目标在所有三个模型上恢复（Study J）。
6. 后缀的抵消效应在Opus 5和Fable 5.1的不同措辞中表现一致（Study H2）。
7. 在第二存储中，所有模型均遵循标准（Study H1）。
8. 标准将Opus 5的选择推向当前记录（+100.0分），而远离Fable 5.1的记录（Study I）。
9. 单字符计划指针效果稳定（+78.0至+81.7分，Study B/B'）。

Conclusion: 指令的具体形式（指针、标准及其组合）对智能体的记录选择有显著且模型特异性的描述性影响。虽然某些格式（如纯标准）可能在特定条件下失效或被抵消，但通过结构化增强（如认证行、预算限制）可有效引导行为。研究强调这些是精确编辑在固定面板上的描述性效应，而非因果机制主张。

Abstract: An agent that inherits six one-line memories may pull at most one archived source record before acting; a directive written into the store can steer that choice: a pointer to the record, a criterion that identifies it, or both. Across twelve registered studies on one instrument lineage (14,760 attempts) we measured where the request goes under each form. On six direct-provider models a length-matched criterion exceeded a bare id by +35.0 points [+31.2, +38.8] (Study D); the contrast failed its registered superiority rule on a nine-model OpenRouter-served panel (Study E). Appending the id cancelled the criterion on three Claude models (Opus 5: 40/40 to 0/40; Study F-x); six byte-matched edits gave each exact string its own effect (Study G), and a re-run at eighty runs per cell left fifteen of thirty replication contrasts within the margin, fifteen unresolved and none beyond (Study G'). A ratification line (+96.0 points on Opus 5) and a budget of two credits restored the target on all three (Study J); across five criterion strings the suffix's cancellation held for four of the five wordings on Opus 5 and all five wordings on Fable 5.1 (Study H2); in a second store every model followed the criterion (Study H1). Continued into a decision, the criterion moved the choice toward the current record (+100.0 points, Opus 5) and away from it on Fable 5.1 (Study I). A one-character plan pointer's effect (+78.0 points; Study B, after a correction of its first repository report) returned the same verdict under a prospectively registered re-run (+81.7 points; Study B'). All results are descriptive effects of exact edits on fixed panels with registered intervals and no mechanism claim.

</details>


### [195] [ExplainRoute: A Pre-Deployment Audit Framework for Non-Answer-Giving Programming Tutors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03470)
*Yiming Gai,Yingying Zhang,Xuefei Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出ExplainRoute框架，用于在部署前审计非直接给答案的编程辅导工具。通过评估学习者解释状态来选择费曼式或苏格拉底式回应，并验证其契约有效性。结果显示自适应路由并未显著优于固定策略，但提供了有效的审计协议和边界发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编程辅导应支持学习者的自我解释而非直接提供模型答案，但目前缺乏在课堂部署前对这类“非直接回答”辅导工具进行有效审计的方法。

Method: 构建ExplainRoute预部署审计框架，输入代码行和学习者解释，估算解释状态并选择两种受限响应之一（费曼式自我解释提示或苏格拉底式脚手架）。该框架通过机器可检查的合同暴露状态、策略、引用代码片段和泄露风险。在SelfCode语料库上进行离线评估，对比直接回答、固定开放自我解释、固定苏格拉底脚手架、自适应路由和无状态消融方案。

Result: 契约在所有教学条件下有效率达到100%。自适应路由在60.5%的记录上与冻结参考规则匹配，状态宏观F1为0.238，未显示可靠的自适应优势。独立语言模型评分显示自适应响应得分4.516/5，低于固定开放自我解释(4.598)和苏格拉底脚手架(4.658)，但高于无状态消融(2.819)。盲评确认可见的学习者解释提升了信息价值，但自适应路由未优于固定策略。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提供了一个经过验证的审计协议和一个边界发现，即证明了自适应路由在此场景下并不比固定策略更优，而非证明其提高了学习效果或因果教学有效性。

Abstract: Programming tutors should support learners' own explanations rather than immediately providing model answers. We present ExplainRoute, a pre-deployment audit framework for non-answer-giving programming tutors. Given a code line and a learner explanation, it estimates the explanation state and selects one of two bounded responses: a Feynman-style self-explanation prompt or a Socratic scaffold. The framework exposes its state, strategy, cited code fragment, and leakage risk through a machine-checkable contract. Unlike benchmarks that rank tutors by fluency alone, ExplainRoute audits information boundaries, response polarity, failure closure, and the value of learner-explanation visibility before classroom deployment. We evaluate it offline on the 1,770-pair SelfCode corpus using a code-group split, with 443 pairs reserved in 11 untouched holdout groups. The evaluation compares direct answers, fixed open self-explanation, fixed Socratic scaffolding, adaptive routing, and an adaptive no-state ablation. Contract validity reaches 100% for all pedagogical conditions. Adaptive routing matches the frozen reference rule on 60.5% of records, with state macro-F1 of 0.238 (Open: 0.229; Socratic: 0.246), showing no reliable adaptive advantage. An independent language-model judge scores adaptive responses 4.516/5, outperforming the no-state ablation (2.819/5) but slightly below fixed open self-explanation (4.598/5) and Socratic scaffolding (4.658/5). A blinded rubric evaluation on a stratified 40-row subset confirms that visible learner explanations improve information value while adaptive routing does not outperform fixed strategies. The contribution is a validated audit protocol and a boundary finding, rather than evidence of improved learning, retention, or causal instructional effectiveness.

</details>


### [196] [From Topical Relevance to Answerability: Entailment Distillation for Conversational Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03482)
*Shuai Qin,Guojia An,Weikang Guo,Pei Ke,Jiwei Wei,Yang Yang,Jie Zou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出CLEAR框架，通过蕴含蒸馏和回溯召回解决对话检索中“话题相关性”与“答案可支持性”不匹配的问题，显著提升检索精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话检索器常将话题相关性作为答案可支持性的代理指标，导致高度匹配上下文但无法支撑正确答案的“系统性答案可支持性差距”。

Method: 提出CLEAR框架：1）核心为蕴含蒸馏，将答案-段落蕴含监督迁移至交叉编码器重排器，无需答案即可区分支持性段落与话题干扰项；2）辅以段落中心回溯召回模块，利用LLM从段落推断可答查询以召回低相似度但可答段落。

Result: 在TopiOCQA、QReCC及TREC CAS等数据集上，CLEAR在顶部排名精度上持续优于强基线（如查询重写和密集检索），在话题噪声较大的对话中提升最显著；结合LLM驱动的查询重写器可进一步增益。

Conclusion: CLEAR通过转向以答案可支持性为核心的检索范式，有效解决了传统方法的话题噪音问题，提升了复杂对话场景下的检索性能。

Abstract: Existing conversational retrievers commonly treat topical relevance as a proxy for answerability. However, a passage that closely matches the dialogue context is not necessarily the one that supports the correct answer. We identify this mismatch as a systematic answerability gap. To address this issue, we propose CLEAR, a framework that shifts conversational retrieval from topical relevance to answerability. The core of CLEAR is entailment distillation, which transfers answer-passage entailment supervision into a cross-encoder reranker so that the reranker discriminates answer-supporting passages from topical distractors at inference time, without requiring answers. CLEAR is complemented by a passage-centric abductive recall module that brings low-similarity yet answerable passages into the candidate pool by inferring answerable queries from passages with an LLM. Across TopiOCQA, QReCC, and out-of-domain TREC CAsT datasets, CLEAR consistently improves top-ranked precision over strong query-rewriting and dense-retrieval baselines, with the largest gains observed in conversations involving heavier topical noise. Moreover, applying our reranker on top of an LLM-driven query rewriter yields further gains.

</details>


### [197] [EPIC: Explicit Posterior Item Conditioning for Semantic ID Diffusion Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03522)
*Tuan-Binh Tran,Thanh Tam Nguyen,Quoc Viet Hung Nguyen,Dung D. Le,Tung Kieu,Thanh Trung Huynh*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出EPIC方法，通过在SID去噪过程中引入显式的项目级竞争机制，利用个性化后验分布指导token预测，从而提升推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的掩码扩散方法在语义ID（SID）生成推荐中主要基于位置式的token预测，导致在去噪步骤中无法有效区分对应多个可行项目的部分SID，缺乏对完整目录项的选择能力。

Method: 提出显式后验项目条件（EPIC），构建基于当前生成上下文和用户近期交互的可行候选项目个性化后验分布，并将该分布投影回未解决的SID位置以指导后续的token决策。该方法冻结预训练主干网络，无需额外的解码器前向传播。

Result: 在四个Amazon基准数据集上实验表明，EPIC相比强基线方法具有一致的性能提升；诊断分析显示，增益主要来源于保留有前景项目假设的个性化转移证据。

Conclusion: EPIC通过引入显式的项目级竞争和个性化后验引导，有效解决了SID去噪中的多义性问题，显著提升了生成式推荐的效果。

Abstract: Semantic ID (SID) generative recommendation predicts the next item by generating a short tuple of discrete tokens. Recent masked-diffusion methods improve this process through bidirectional context and flexible decoding, yet recommendation ultimately requires selecting among complete catalog items. At each denoising step, a partial SID can correspond to multiple feasible items, while existing methods primarily reason through position-wise token predictions. We propose Explicit Posterior Item Conditioning (EPIC), which introduces explicit item-level competition into SID denoising. EPIC constructs a personalized posterior over feasible candidate items using the current generation context and the user's recent interactions, then projects this distribution back to unresolved SID positions to guide subsequent token decisions. The pretrained backbone remains frozen and requires no additional decoder forward pass. Experiments on four Amazon benchmarks show consistent improvements over strong baselines, while diagnostic analyses indicate that the gains primarily arise from personalized transition evidence that preserves promising item hypotheses during denoising.

</details>


### [198] [LLM4AIGQ: LLM-based AI Guidance Query Generation Framework for Multi Interest Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03674)
*Xiangchen Pan,Jiayi Xu,Jing Wang,Xing Fang,Lingyun Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出LLM4AIGQ，利用大语言模型解决传统AIGQ生成的语义漂移和用户兴趣单一问题，通过多兴趣分割、SFT/RL/DPO训练及多级奖励机制，实现高质量AI引导查询生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于“用户-物品”共现关系的两阶段检索方法存在信息级联丢失导致的语义漂移，且缺乏对用户多重兴趣的探索，导致生成的引导查询价值低、购买意图不匹配。

Method: 提出LLM4AIGQ框架：1) 整合用户画像和历史交互序列以分割用户兴趣并推断子兴趣消费意图；2) 采用包含监督微调(SFT)、强化学习(RL)和直接偏好优化(DPO)的后训练流水线；3) 设计多级奖励机制以支持多目标优化和长链推理；4) 采用近线生成与在线读取架构以满足延迟要求。

Result: 离线评估和线上A/B测试均显示该模型具有稳健的性能，有效提升了AI引导查询的质量和相关性。

Conclusion: 基于大语言模型的LLM4AIGQ方案能有效克服传统方法的局限性，通过精细化用户兴趣建模和优化训练策略，显著提升了电商场景中AI引导查询的生成效果和商业价值。

Abstract: Guidance queries stimulate user consumption by extracting preferences to provide search queries with guidance value, playing a crucial role in the e-commerce field. Traditional AI-generated queries (AIGQ) generation primarily relies on a two-stage "Query-to-AI-Generated-Query" (Q2AIGQ) association paradigm, first recalling user primary search queries from user profiles, historical behavior sequences, item-side information, and the current query through multi-path retrieval, then generalizing AIGQ via rule-based methods. This approach suffers from semantic drift due to information cascade loss; additionally, primary search query derivation heavily depends on "user-item" co-occurrence relationships, lacking exploration of user multi-interests, resulting in guidance queries with low value and mismatched purchase intent. To address the expressive limitations of traditional co-occurrence-based retrieval, we propose LLM4AIGQ, an LLM-based solution for generating AI guidance queries tailored to users' multi-interests. This approach segments user interests by integrating user profiles and historical interaction sequences, infers specific consumption intents for each sub-interest, and subsequently generates corresponding AIGQ. In terms of model training, we employ a post-training pipeline comprising Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), Reinforcement Learning (RL), and Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to enhance the model's capability in generating AIGQ. We also introduce a multi-level reward design to satisfy the requirements of multi-objective optimization and long-chain reasoning in practical applications. Regarding deployment, we adopt a nearline-generation and online-read architecture to meet latency constraints. Extensive experimental analyses demonstrate that our model achieves robust performance in both offline evaluations and online A/B tests.

</details>


### [199] [Unified Pitch Graphs for Diagnosing Pitching Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03810)
*Kichang Lee,JeongGil Ko*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出统一投球图（UPG），一种分层图表示法，用于保留每颗球的精确事件、3D轨迹及上下文，通过支持自适应机制提升长序列覆盖率和重建精度，有效识别离散表示无法捕捉的策略模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有棒球投球策略表征方法将投球简化为离散类型或聚合统计量，忽略了物理执行细节和有序上下文中的时序事件，导致信息丢失且无法区分名义上相同但执行不同的投球序列。

Method: 构建统一投球图（UPG），作为分层图结构：1）保留每颗投球为独立事件，重构三维轨迹与上下文；2）通过有向边连接连续投球以体现序列关系；3）在语义和时间分辨率上组织事件；4）引入支持自适应机制，在证据不足时从细粒度长序列回退，同时保持事件 lineage。使用2021-2026年MLB Statcast数据（394万颗球）进行评估。

Result: 1）名义相同的投球序列显示出不同的物理执行，长上下文路径中有序结构更明显；2）支持自适应回退机制将未见路径覆盖率从18.9%提升至94.9%，执行重建R²从0.495提升至0.685；3）UPG能可靠定位离散投球混合和序列表示无法检测到的受控执行变化。

Conclusion: UPG提供了一种可追溯的多尺度表征，能够识别 recurring 策略模式，同时避免将回顾性关联与因果或未来表现主张混淆，优于传统离散或聚合表征方法。

Abstract: Pitching strategy in baseball is expressed through both physical execution and the ordered context in which pitches are used, yet common representations collapse pitches into discrete types or aggregate statistics. We present Unified Pitch Graphs (UPG), a hierarchical graph representation for retrospective analysis of sequential spatiotemporal events. UPG preserves each pitch as an exact event with reconstructed three-dimensional trajectory and context, connects consecutive pitches through directed sequence edges, and organizes the same events across semantic and temporal resolutions. A support-adaptive mechanism backs off from fine, long sequences when repeated evidence is insufficient, while retaining exact event lineage. We evaluate UPG on 3.94 million MLB Statcast pitches from 2021 to 2026. Nominally identical pitch sequences exhibit distinct physical executions, and ordered structure becomes increasingly evident in longer context-conditioned paths. Support-adaptive backoff increases held-out path coverage from 18.9\% to 94.9\% while improving execution reconstruction from $R^2=0.495$ to $0.685$. UPG also reliably localizes controlled execution changes that discrete pitch-mix and sequence representations cannot detect. These results demonstrate that UPG provides a traceable, multi-scale representation for identifying recurring strategy patterns without conflating retrospective associations with causal or future-performance claims.

</details>


### [200] [GRASP: Graph-Retrieval Automated Scoring Pipeline for Label-Free Multi-Topic Essay Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03857)
*Aafreen Husain,Samar Shailendra,Saad Sajid Hashmi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出GRASP框架，通过图检索增强生成解决无标签多主题科学考试自动评分问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦单主题考试，针对包含多个主题且无标记分割的“无标签”考试的自动评分研究较少。

Method: 构建FAISS向量索引和语义相似度图；利用大语言模型和学生作文句子数启发式方法预测主题数量；结合基于余弦相似度的RAG与图遍历的GRAG检索参考节点；使用匈牙利算法优化分配参考答案；最后用GPT-4.1-mini独立评分。

Result: 实验验证了检索质量对评分准确性的影响，并证明了在复杂程度不同的文章中，图增强检索（GRAG）优于严格的余弦相似度方法。

Conclusion: 该流水线无需训练数据或领域特定示例，能有效处理多主题合并的短答案考试，提升了自动评分的效果。

Abstract: Automated short-answer grading research has historically focused on exams consisting solely of questions pertaining to a single topic. Automatic grading of exams containing questions about more than one topic remains less explored. In this work, a Graph-Retrieval Automated Scoring Pipeline (GRASP) is introduced for grading label-free multi-topic science exams. Label-free exams are short-answer exams in which a student's responses to several distinct topics are merged into a single paragraph, with no markup labels or segmentation indicating which span answers which question. Reference answers for each question are encoded into a FAISS vector index via Sentence-BERT, and a semantic similarity graph is constructed over this set of reference answers. At grading time, sentence count heuristics, with a large language model used to resolve ambiguous cases, are first applied to predict how many distinct topics were answered in the student essay. This process is performed without training data or domain-specific example essays. Candidate reference nodes, each storing one (question, reference answer, concatenation of both) from the reference index, are then retrieved through cosine similarity based Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation (GRAG). GRAG operates by taking the top cosine matches as seed nodes and then performing a graph traversal over strong edges to find additional reference nodes that may have been missed by RAG. The Hungarian algorithm is then used to optimally assign one reference node per question segment such that no reference is duplicated. Each segment is then graded against its assigned reference independently using GPT-4.1-mini. This experiment is performed to show the effect of retrieval quality on grading accuracy and the benefit of graph-augmented retrieval versus strict cosine similarity methods at various levels of essay complexity.

</details>


### [201] [Comparing Retrieval Methods for Academic Advisor Discovery: A Six-Method Study of 768 CS Faculty Profiles Across 9 US Universities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03901)
*Biraj Subedi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文评估了六种学术导师发现方法，结果显示基于重排序（Reranked）的方法在NDCG@10指标上表现最佳（0.477），显著优于传统稀疏匹配方法。研究发现仅使用传记信息比结合研究标签更有效，且引入arXiv摘要会降低性能，从而支持晚期融合架构。所有数据代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对研究生根据研究兴趣陈述寻找合适计算机科学导师的任务，对现有的六种信息检索方法进行全面的比较评估，以识别最有效的方法并优化检索策略。

Method: 构建了包含9所美国大学CS系768位教师档案的新领域数据集，并通过5个查询进行了162次相关性判断。评估了六种方法：Jaccard、TF-IDF、BM25（稀疏词汇匹配）、all-MiniLM-L6-v2（密集语义检索）、混合分数融合以及学习排序（Learning-to-rank）。采用NDCG@10作为主要评价指标，并进行Bonferroni校正的统计显著性检验及消融实验。

Result: Reranked方法取得最高平均NDCG@10为0.477，其次为Semantic (0.450)、Hybrid (0.421)、BM25 (0.406)、Jaccard (0.303)和TF-IDF (0.246)。统计检验显示TF-IDF显著差于其他四种方法。消融实验表明，仅使用传记信息（NDCG 0.634）优于结合传记与研究标签的全模型（0.593）；加入arXiv论文摘要导致NDCG@10下降0.176。

Conclusion: 在学术导师发现任务中，高级重排序和语义检索方法优于传统的词法匹配方法。数据预处理策略对性能有重大影响：单纯依赖传记文本效果最佳，而盲目增加研究标签或外部文献摘要可能引入噪声降低精度，这提示应采用更精细的晚期融合架构而非简单拼接。

Abstract: We present a comparative evaluation of six information retrieval methods for the task of academic advisor discovery: ranking CS faculty members by relevance to a graduate applicant's research interest statement. The methods span sparse lexical matching (Jaccard overlap, TF-IDF, BM25), dense semantic retrieval (all-MiniLM-L6-v2 sentence embeddings), hybrid score fusion, and learning-to-rank. Evaluation uses a new domain-specific collection: 768 faculty profiles scraped from 9 US CS departments, with 162 graded relevance judgments (grade 0/1/2) across 5 queries representing distinct graduate student research profiles. Across all five queries, Reranked achieves the highest mean NDCG@10 (0.477, std 0.138), followed by Semantic (0.450), Hybrid (0.421), BM25 (0.406), Jaccard (0.303), and TF-IDF (0.246). After Bonferroni correction across all 15 pairwise comparisons, TF-IDF is significantly worse than BM25, Semantic, Hybrid, and Reranked; no other pairwise difference survives correction at 5 queries. A field ablation reveals that biography alone (NDCG 0.634) outperforms the full model combining biography with research area tags (0.593). A controlled experiment shows that concatenating arXiv paper abstracts reduces NDCG@10 by 0.176, motivating a late-fusion architecture. All code, scrapers, and relevance labels are released openly.

</details>


### [202] [The WebKurator.de Platform: Combined Regional and Topical Web Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03971)
*Michael Dinzinger,Natanael Arndt,Ben Böck,Jelena Mitrović,Michael Granitzer*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了WebKurator.de，一个用于德国网页的协作式区域与主题双重分类平台。它通过分离地理和主题维度，结合LLM分类、地址提取和人工审核，从554万个网站中构建了包含258万个德国网站的基础数据集，旨在解决传统目录式网页归档缺乏地理维度的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于目录的网页归档方法（如Curlie）主要采用以主题为中心的一维层级结构，导致地理方面与主题及语言类别交织在一起，难以有效保存具有文化和地区相关性的内容。国家图书馆和记忆机构面临系统化整理网络内容的挑战，需要一种能明确区分区域和主题的归档方式。

Method: 1. 提出二维归档模型：明确分离主题分类和地理标注。
2. 技术整合：使用基于大语言模型（LLM）的主题分类，以及基于版权声明页（imprint pages）的地址提取与地理编码技术。
3. 协作机制：支持用户建议并结合 moderated review（审核机制）。
4. 数据启动：基于“德国版权声明数据集”（German Imprints Dataset），该数据集包含554万个网站，从中提取并地理编码了314万个含有版权声明页的网站。

Result: 在314万个含有版权声明页的网站中，成功识别出258万个（占比85.17%）位于德国且拥有分配的主题标签的网站。这些网站构成了WebKurator.de的初始基础，并可随用户建议持续扩展。

Conclusion: WebKurator.de通过引入二维归档模型和自动化数据处理流程，为德国网页提供了更精细的区域和主题分类基础，解决了传统方法中地理信息缺失或混淆的问题，为文化遗产机构的网络归档提供了新的协作式解决方案。

Abstract: The systematic curation of the Web remains a central challenge for national libraries and memory institutions that aim to preserve culturally and regionally relevant content. Existing directory-based approaches such as Curlie implement a predominantly topic-centric, one-dimensional hierarchy, where geographic aspects are intertwined with topical and linguistic categories. To address this limitation, we present WebKurator.de, a collaborative platform for combined regional and topical web curation, initially focused on the German web. WebKurator introduces a two-dimensional curation model that explicitly separates topical categorization and geographic annotation. The system integrates LLM-based topic classification and imprint-based address extraction with geocoding, and supports user suggestions together with moderated review. The platform is bootstrapped from the German Imprints Dataset, a large-scale collection of 5.54 million websites. Among them, 3.14 million contain imprint pages, for which we successfully extracted and geocoded postal addresses. Of these, 2.58 million (85.17%) are located in Germany and also have an assigned topic label. These websites form the initial foundation of WebKurator.de and can be continuously extended through user suggestions.

</details>


### [203] [The Dice Roll Method: A Standardized Protocol for Repeated-Query Auditing of Large Language Model Brand Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04047)
*Dmitrij Żatuchin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出“骰子掷法”协议，通过负二项混合模型等统计方法分解LLM推荐结果的方差，确立了基于通用性理论的迭代次数指导标准（探索5次、确认10次、严谨15次），为LLM品牌推荐的重复查询审计提供了统计学依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前研究人员在审计大型语言模型（LLM）品牌推荐时缺乏标准化协议，特别是在设置迭代次数、选择稳定性指标和建立可靠性阈值方面存在不确定性。

Method: 形式化“骰子掷法”作为可复用的审计协议；将总响应方差分解为采样、提示措辞、运行间和模型版本组件；采用负二项混合模型、Cliff's delta效应量、保留依赖性的Bootstrap及广义理论分解等方法；重新分析了包含约19万观测值的五项品牌推荐审计研究。

Result: 确定了三个层级的迭代指导：探索性(n=5, G=0.58)、确认性(n=10, G=0.74)和严谨性(n=15, G=0.81)；发现四类度量指标互补，建议组合使用；外部验证在39个单元格中有37个成功复现了可靠性预测，但固定层级未直接转移，支持“先试点后解决”的方法。

Conclusion: 该协议为LLM品牌推荐的重复查询审计奠定了统计学基础，适应了真实自回归生成过程中条件非高斯结构的特性。

Abstract: Background: Researchers increasingly use repeated identical prompts to audit stochastic variation in large language model (LLM) brand recommendations, yet no standardized protocol exists for setting iteration counts, selecting stability metrics, or establishing reliability thresholds. Objective: We formalize the Dice Roll Method as a reusable protocol for repeated-query auditing of LLM brand recommendations, grounded in a generative model of temperature-scaled nucleus sampling. Methods: Total response variance is decomposed into sampling, prompt-phrasing, run-to-run, and model-version components. The stack: a negative-binomial mixed model with iterations as repeated measures; Cliff's delta as the distribution-free effect size; dependence-preserving bootstrap; simulation-based power; a generalizability-theory decomposition; drift diagnostics on pinned snapshots. We reanalyse five brand-recommendation auditing studies: approximately 190,000 observations, 270+ brands, 6 languages, iteration counts 5 to 40. Results: Three tiers of iteration guidance emerge from the D-study: exploratory (n = 5, G = 0.58), confirmatory (n = 10, G = 0.74), and rigorous (n = 15, G = 0.81), tied to effect-size and generalizability targets. The four metric families (count, set, embedding, fairness-adjusted PASOR) are complementary, motivating a compact metric battery over single indicators. A pre-registered external validation on three independent corpora (Motoki et al., 100-round; Rozado, 24 models; llm-stability) reproduces the D-study reliability prediction in 37 of 39 cells with no failures and the n = 5 power value to two decimals; the fixed tiers do not transfer, supporting a pilot-then-solve reading. Conclusion: The protocol gives repeated-query auditing of LLM brand recommendations a statistically principled footing under the conditional, non-Gaussian structure of real autoregressive generation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [204] [Structure and Implementation of New Practical English Textbooks Driven by Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02981)
*Ya Wang,Lei Zhang,Xueguang Yang,Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出并验证了一种基于人工智能的实用英语教材原型。该教材采用五层架构，在186名非英语专业本科生中进行了为期八周的测试。结果显示，与静态数字教材相比，该系统显著提高了单元完成准确率（72.4%至84.9%）、口语任务平均分（提升10.8分），并减少了教师批改时间（31.6%）。结论表明，AI驱动教材能在保持课程稳定性的同时提供个性化学习路径和可追踪数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能的发展，应用英语材料正从固定的纸质序列转变为能够诊断学习者、推荐任务和提供形成性反馈的自适应学习系统。旨在研究这种由AI驱动的实用英语教材的结构及其实际应用效果。

Method: 提出一种包含知识图谱、学习者画像、任务生成、反馈编排和教师端治理的五层架构。构建原型系统，并对186名非英语专业本科生进行为期八周的教学实验，将其与静态数字教材进行对比分析。

Result: 测试显示，AI驱动系统的单元完成准确率从72.4%提升至84.9%；口语任务平均分数提高10.8分；教师批改时间减少31.6%。

Conclusion: AI驱动的教材能够在维持课程稳定性的基础上，提供个性化的学习路径、丰富的练习材料以及可追溯的课堂数据，有效提升了教学效果和教学效率。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is changing the form of applied English materials from fixed paper sequences to adaptive learning systems that can diagnose learners, recommend tasks, and provide formative feedback. This paper studies the structure and application of a new practical English textbook driven by artificial intelligence. A five-layer architecture is proposed: knowledge mapping, learner profiling, task generation, feedback orchestration, and teacher-side governance. A prototype was tested on 186 non-English-major undergraduates for eight weeks of teaching. Compared with a static digital textbook, the proposed system increased the unit completion accuracy from 72.4% to 84.9%, raised the average score for speaking tasks by 10.8 points, and reduced the teacher's correction time by 31.6%. Therefore, an AI-driven textbook can maintain the stability of the curriculum while providing personalised learning paths, rich practice materials and traceable classroom data.

</details>


### [205] [MasterControl Seventeen Every Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03209)
*MasterControl AI Lab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了一种受控的企业分析方法，通过语言模型解释问题，而确定性策略选择并运行预批准的分析程序。实验表明，在特定分析类中，这种限制仍具有表达能力，且固定规则使结果可重放。Qwen3-8B模型在意图解释和策略执行方面表现优异，匹配了所有测试数据集的完整答案和证据合同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在企业分析环境中，如何通过结合语言模型的灵活性和确定性策略的可靠性，来确保分析结果的准确性和可重复性。

Method: 采用一种混合方法：语言模型负责解释用户问题，而确定性策略则选择并运行预批准的分析程序。该方法使用了关系操作、聚合、比较、窗口、排名和相似度等分析功能，并通过固定的意义、策略、数据和执行规则来保证结果的可重放性。实验涉及440次运行，对比了不同模型的表现。

Result: 在440次运行中，三个8B模型在生成SQL和运行时选择工具方面表现出色，但Qwen3-8B仅在意图解释上发挥作用，由策略执行批准的程序。在330个运行时规划事件中，没有一个完全匹配所有测试数据集的答案和证据合同，但策略执行的分析仪在所有110个测试中都成功匹配。

Conclusion: 这种受控方法在特定的分析类和配置下是有效的，能够确保结果的准确性和可重复性。然而，这一结果是特定于配置的，不能直接推广到其他设计或场景中。

Abstract: We study a governed approach to enterprise analytics: a language model interprets the question, while deterministic policy selects and runs a pre-approved analytical program that returns both results and evidence. We show that this restriction can remain expressive within a defined analytical class, using relational operations plus aggregation, comparison, windows, ranking, and similarity. Fixed meaning, policy, data, and execution rules also make results replayable. Across 440 runs, three 8B models generated SQL and selected tools at runtime, while Qwen3-8B interpreted intent only and policy executed the approved program. None of 330 runtime-planning episodes matched the full answer-and-evidence contract across all test datasets; the policy-executed analyzer matched 110 of 110. This is a configuration-specific result, not evidence that runtime agents cannot succeed under other designs.

</details>


### [206] [Speculative Macro Commit for Faster Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03236)
*Zeyu Liu,Souvik Kundu,Peter A. Beerel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出推测宏提交（SMC）机制，通过两级代理系统（权威模型+推测草稿模型）复用多步动作链，显著降低LLM工具使用代理的延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决LLM代理在串行“动作-观察”回合中因工具调用和环境转换导致的推理延迟问题。

Method: 构建两级代理系统：权威大模型生成官方轨迹，快速小模型在隔离环境中预测并执行未来动作链；从训练数据提取多步动作骨架存入宏库，运行时匹配草稿动作，若首步一致则提交预执行的后续步骤及观察结果。

Result: 基于Qwen3.5系列模型，在$τ^2$-Bench电信子集上准确率持平，延迟较Speculative Actions基线降低10.23%，较顺序执行降低18.59%；在AppWorld上延迟降低7.7%-44.9%，任务完成率略有下降。

Conclusion: SMC提供了一种实用的多步推测执行复用方法，能有效减少代理延迟，优于单步推测行动。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents spend wall-clock time not only on model inference but also in serial action--observation turns, where each tool call, environment transition, and observation can delay subsequent decisions. We introduce \textbf{Speculative Macro Commit} (SMC), a runtime mechanism for a two-tier agent system: a large authoritative actor model produces the official trajectory, while a faster speculative drafter model continuously predicts and executes future action chains on an isolated environment snapshot. SMC mines recurring multi-action skeletons from training traces and stores them in a macro library used to match against action chains predicted by the drafter at runtime. When the actor's next tool call matches the first drafted action, SMC commits the remaining pre-executed draft steps, together with their observations, to the official trajectory. Using Qwen3.5-27B INT4 as the authoritative actor model and Qwen3.5-4B as the speculative drafter model, SMC matches the sequential agent's overall accuracy while reducing latency by 10.23\% over the Speculative Actions (SA) baseline and 18.59\% over sequential execution on the $τ^2$-Bench Telecom subset. On AppWorld, SMC reduces wall time by 7.7\% over SA baseline and 44.9\% over sequential execution, with a small reduction in task completion. Overall, SMC provides a practical way to reuse multi-step speculative execution and reduce agent latency beyond single-step speculative actions. Our code is publicly available \href{https://github.com/zeyuliu1037/speculative-macro-commit}{\textcolor{magenta}{here}}.

</details>


### [207] [Fresh Memory, Stale Plans: Dependency-Scoped Validation for Distributed LLM-Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03340)
*Evan Chen,Shiqiang Wang,Christopher G. Brinton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PlanFence协议解决分布式LLM智能体团队中的“过时计划执行”问题，通过依赖范围的动作验证确保执行有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式LLM智能体系统中，即使状态是最新的，智能体仍可能基于过时的计划行动（称为stale-plan execution），导致动作失效。

Method: 引入PlanFence，一种依赖范围的动作验证协议。计划引用具体的公共记录，执行器仅验证影响待处理外部动作的记录，若验证不完整则重新规划或阻塞。

Result: 在30个受控实时工作流中，仅靠状态新鲜度的执行器在所有任务中均执行了过时计划；而PlanFence完成了所有任务且无无效动作。重放显示：低变更率下主动同步减少协调停滞，高变更率/大键空间下PlanFence避免重复更新路径协调及无关状态验证。

Conclusion: PlanFence提供了受控的安全性和系统成本效益，而非通用任务准确性提升，有效解决了状态新鲜度无法保证计划有效性的问题。

Abstract: Distributed LLM-agent teams can read the latest shared facts and still act on an obsolete plan. A planner may derive an action from requirement $r_3$, another agent may commit $r_4$, and an executor may receive $r_4$ without replacing the plan derived from $r_3$. We call this \emph{stale-plan execution}: state freshness does not establish that the plan authorizing an action remains valid. We introduce PlanFence, a dependency-scoped action-validation protocol. Plans cite the exact public records they used, and an executor validates only the records that can affect the pending external action, replanning once or blocking when validation is incomplete. In 30 controlled live workflows with a post-plan revision, a freshness-only executor acts on the obsolete plan in every task, whereas PlanFence completes all tasks without an invalid action. Controlled replay reveals two conditional boundaries: proactive synchronization yields lower coordination stall at low churn, while PlanFence avoids repeated update-path coordination as churn grows and avoids validating unrelated state as the shared keyspace grows. These are controlled safety and systems-cost results, not general task-accuracy gains.

</details>


### [208] [A Prompt-Engineering Approach to Develop Scalable, Flexible, and Real-Time Hybrid Micro-Level Personalization in a General Purpose AI Teaching Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03402)
*Saptarshi Basu,Sandeep Kakar,Ashok Goel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于提示工程的框架，通过6个学习者维度（自评、抽象偏好等）和布鲁姆分类法分析认知复杂度，为LLM/RAG教学助手生成96种学习者画像并定制提示，无需重训练即可实现个性化响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有由大语言模型驱动的AI教学助手虽具备可扩展性，但缺乏足够的个性化支持。

Method: 构建提示工程框架，利用6个学习者特定维度和布鲁姆分类法将学习者属性与认知评估编码为结构化提示，以条件化LLM响应；通过NLP指标实验和5人人类研究进行评估。

Result: 不同个性化条件下响应的风格和结构存在感知差异；统计分析识别出与可测量响应变化相关的学习者属性；初步证明基于提示的个性化可支持自适应行为。

Conclusion: 基于提示的个性化方法能有效增强LLM驱动的教育代理的适应性行为，为通用LLM在教学场景中的个性化应用提供证据。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) teaching assistants powered by large language models (LLMs) offer scalable educational support but often provide limited personalization. This study presents a prompt-engineering-based framework for personalizing general-purpose LLM/RAG-based AI teaching assistants such as Jill Watson across academic disciplines and courses. The framework adapts responses using six learner-specific dimensions: self-assessment, abstraction preference, verbosity preference, perceptual orientation, information processing style, and level of understanding, yielding 96 distinct learner profiles. Student queries are additionally analyzed using Bloom's Taxonomy to estimate cognitive complexity at the interaction level. Learner attributes and cognitive assessments are encoded in structured prompts that condition the LLM without requiring model retraining. The framework is evaluated through experiments using NLP metrics and a human study with five participants. Results show perceived differences in response style and structure across personalization conditions, with statistical analyses identifying learner attributes associated with measurable response changes. These findings provide preliminary evidence that prompt-based personalization can support adaptive behavior in LLM-powered educational agents.

</details>


### [209] [Caught in the Story: Narrative Captivity in Multi-turn LLMs Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03407)
*Yuhe Wu,Guangyu Wang,Yujie Chen,Jiatong Zhang,Yuran Chen,Yutong Zhang,Xiyin Cheng,Wenpeng Cao,Zhuang Liu,Guang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究揭示了大型语言模型（LLM）在道德咨询中存在的“叙事俘获”现象，即模型易受单方面叙述影响而丧失独立判断。通过构建包含5078个场景的基准测试，发现多轮单向叙述导致模型判决平均偏移25个百分点，偏好优化是主要成因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多基于单轮判断或对抗性反驳来评估LLM的道德推理，这与现实世界中寻求指导时往往只有一方提供自我辩护的多轮、非对称对话场景不符。需要探究在没有明确对立立场的情况下，单纯的叙述是否会导致模型判断偏差。

Method: 提出“叙事俘获”概念并构建涵盖六个道德维度的5,078个 interpersonal-conflict 场景基准。对17种LLM进行多轮叙事与单基线对比实验，并通过阶段级分析识别偏好优化等关键影响因素及推理时策略的效果。

Result: “叙事俘获”在17种LLM中普遍存在。在多轮叙述下，模型的最终判决相比单轮基线平均偏移了25个百分点。分析表明，偏好优化是导致该现象的主要因素，而四种推理时策略仅能提供部分缓解作用。

Conclusion: 现实世界中的道德冲突对话常产生信息不对称，LLM容易将单方面的自我辩护视为完整事实并与其对齐。研究呼吁开发能在真实咨询中保持独立判断能力的LLM顾问系统。

Abstract: People increasingly turn to large language models (LLMs) for everyday advice, making ethically charged interpersonal problems a practical moral-advisory context. Most prior work has studied this context through single-turn judgments or pressure-laden rebuttals, assumptions that poorly match how guidance is sought in real-world contexts. These assumptions leave unclear whether narration alone, without an explicit opposing position, can shift model judgments during multi-turn moral consultation. Yet real-world moral-conflict conversation often elicits one party's self-justifying account, which can unfold over multiple turns and create information asymmetry. We introduce \textbf{narrative captivity}, a failure mode in which a model treats an unopposed one-sided account as complete and aligns with the narrator's interpretation without seeking missing perspectives. To measure this phenomenon, we build a benchmark of $5{,}078$ interpersonal-conflict scenarios spanning six moral dimensions. Across 17 LLMs, narrative captivity is widespread: end-state judgments under multi-turn narration shift by 25 percentage points on average beyond the matched single-turn baseline. Stage-level analysis identifies preference optimization as a major contributor, while four inference-time strategies provide only partial mitigation. We hope our project fosters LLM advisors that preserve independent judgment in real-world consultation.

</details>


### [210] [Dude: A Dual-Detection Multi-Agent System for Paper-Code Discrepancy Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03416)
*Weijie Liu,Running Zhao,Wenhao Yuan,Jinfeng Xu,Zhanfeng Xu,Xiaoxi Zhang,Edith Cheuk-Han Ngai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Dude，首个双检测多智能体系统，通过粒度对齐协商和两阶段显著性过滤机制，解决论文-代码差异检测中的误报问题，显著提升召回率和精确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着研究提交量超过人工审查能力，LLM赋能的论文-代码差异检测受到关注。但现有单智能体LLM存在上下文容量有限和检测片面性问题，导致差异检测的召回性能低下。

Method: 提出Dude（Dual-Detection Multi-Agent System），针对论文语言与代码语言间的粒度不对称导致的过度解释和过度报告挑战，设计了粒度对齐协商机制和两阶段显著性过滤机制，以抑制多智能体系统中的假阳性。

Result: 在真实世界论文-代码差异数据集上的实验表明，Dude相比基线方法，召回率和精确率提升高达22.8%，F1分数提升高达18.7%。

Conclusion: Dude通过解决粒度不对称带来的误报问题，有效提升了论文-代码差异检测的性能，为大规模研究提交的自动化审查提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: LLM-empowered paper-code discrepancy detection has received growing concern since the scaling of research submissions exceeds the manual review capability. However, the limited context capacity and one-sided discrepancy detection of existing single-agent LLM paradigms lead to an inferior recall performance in detecting discrepancies. In this paper, we propose Dude, the first Dual-Detection Multi-Agent System for paper-code discrepancy detection. We discover that the granularity asymmetry of the paper-language and code-language introduces over-interpretation and over-reporting challenges in a multi-agent system design for discrepancy detection, resulting in increasing false positives. To address this, we propose a granularity-aligned negotiation and a two-stage salience-filtering mechanism in Dude, which effectively prevents agents from falsely reporting discrepancies. Experimental results in real-world paper-code discrepancy datasets showcase Dude's significant recall and precision improvement by up to 22.8%, increasing F1 score by up to 18.7% compared to baseline methods.

</details>


### [211] [DuplexSpeechBench-IFEval: Evaluating Implicit Instruction Following in Full-Duplex Voice Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03423)
*Puneet Mathur,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了DuplexSpeechBench-IFEval (DSB-IFEval)，一个用于评估全双工语音代理在实时口语交互中隐式指令遵循能力的基准测试。研究揭示了不同架构模型在显式指令与角色隐含行为之间的权衡，发现基于角色的行为推断和执行、以及解决冲突指令仍是主要挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基准测试主要通过显式的轮次管理指令来测试语音代理的行为，而部署中的代理通常通过角色或人格进行配置，需要从这些设定中推断出适当的对话行为。为了填补这一差距，需要评估代理在隐式指令遵循方面的能力。

Method: 提出了DSB-IFEval基准，包含1,038个测试用例，涵盖八种不同的助手角色。评估了五种条件协议：默认行为、显式行为指令、角色隐含行为、组合角色-规则条件以及指令冲突。使用确定性指令遵循分数（IAS）衡量实时话语权管理，使用LLM判断的角色遵循分数（PAS）衡量内容一致性。对六种实时语音系统进行了测试。

Result: 发现架构依赖的权衡：全双工模型（如F-Actor和PersonaPlex）对指令是显式还是从角色推断更敏感，仅在角色条件下的依从性分别下降9.7%和4.5%。相比之下，GPT-Realtime、MiniCPM-o和Fun-Audio-Chat强于保持角色一致的内容，但其话语权行为在显式和仅角色指令间缺乏适应性，且在主动行为上受限。此外，即使在安全冲突下，系统也难以覆盖与其指定角色相符的冲突指令。

Conclusion: 推断角色隐含的行为、在适当的对话时刻执行该行为，以及解决竞争性指令，仍然是全双工语音代理面临的独特挑战。

Abstract: Full-duplex voice agents must continuously decide when to listen, backchannel, interrupt, handle speech overlaps, take the floor, and yield. Existing benchmarks largely test these behaviors through explicit turn-management instructions, while deployed agents are often configured through roles or personas from which the appropriate conversational behavior must be inferred. We introduce DuplexSpeechBench-IFEval (DSB-IFEval) for evaluating implicit instruction-following in real-time spoken interaction. (DSB-IFEval) comprises 1,038 test cases spanning eight diverse assistant roles and evaluates five conditioning protocols for instruction-following: default behavior, explicit behavioral instructions, persona-implied behavior, combined persona--rule conditioning, and instruction conflict. We measure real-time floor management using a deterministic Instruction Adherence Score (IAS) and persona-consistent content using LLM-judged Persona Adherence Score (PAS). Across six real-time speech systems, we find architecture-dependent trade-offs. Full duplex models like F-Actor and PersonaPlex are more sensitive to whether conversational behavior is stated explicitly or must be inferred from a persona, with adherence dropping by 9.7% and 4.5%, respectively, under persona-only conditioning. In contrast, GPT-Realtime, MiniCPM-o, and Fun-Audio-Chat strongly adhere to persona-consistent content, but their floor behavior does not adapt across explicit and persona-only instructions and remains constrained on several proactive actions. We further find that even if systems reliably follow conflicting directives to their prescribed persona, they still struggle to override them under safety conflict. These results show that inferring the behavior implied by a role, executing it at the appropriate conversational moment, and resolving competing instructions remain distinct challenges for full-duplex voice agents.

</details>


### [212] [Do GUI Agents Know When Not to Act? Enabling Conflict-Aware Termination for Multimodal GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03438)
*Zhaoyuan Huang,Tianjie Ju,Pengzhou Cheng,Zheng Wu,Yansi Li,Chuanbiao Song,Jun Lan,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Zhuosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CONFLICTGUI基准和CONFLICTGUARD框架，解决GUI智能体在遇到冲突指令时盲目执行的问题，通过推理时干预提升其终止决策能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实用户可能因善意错误发出不可行指令，现有GUI智能体虽能执行可行任务，但在面临指令内部或指令与GUI上下文冲突时，往往表现出严重的“执行偏差过度顺从”，即盲目继续执行而非适时停止。

Method: 1. 构建CONFLICTGUI基准，涵盖指令内部冲突和指令-GUI上下文冲突，用于研究冲突感知终止。2. 提出CONFLICTGUARD推理时框架，包含：可行性验证协议（指导智能体在行动前评估指令逻辑和GUI证据）和条件动作调制机制（引导智能体从过度顺从转向终止行为）。

Result: 在五个广泛使用的智能体上实验表明，CONFLICTGUARD显著提高了平均冲突任务成功率，同时保持了正常GUI任务的性能。轻量级推理时干预有效增强了智能体识别不当执行场景并避免不必要行动的能力。

Conclusion: GUI智能体不仅需要知道如何行动，还需知道何时不行动。通过引入冲突感知终止机制和推理时干预，可显著提升智能体在处理不可行或冲突指令时的鲁棒性和可靠性。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) agents are increasingly used to execute natural-language instructions on user interfaces, yet real users may issue infeasible instructions due to benign mistakes. A reliable agent should not only know how to act, but also when not to act. In this work, we introduce CONFLICTGUI, a benchmark covering instruction-internal conflicts and instruction-GUI context conflicts to study conflict-aware termination. Our evaluation reveals severe execution-biased overcompliance: agents that perform well on feasible tasks often continue to execute blindly under conflicting instructions. To mitigate this behavior, we propose CONFLICTGUARD, an inference-time framework that aligns an agent's feasibility awareness with its action generation. CONFLICTGUARD contains two coupled components: a feasibility verification protocol that guides the agent to assess instruction logic and GUI-side evidence before acting, and a conditional action modulation mechanism that steers agents from over-compliant execution into termination-oriented behavior. Experiments across five widely-used agents demonstrate that CONFLICTGUARD improves average conflict task success rate significantly, while preserving normal GUI-task performance. These results validate that a lightweight inference-time intervention can substantially boost GUI Agent's competence to identify inappropriate execution scenarios and refrain from unnecessary actions.

</details>


### [213] [Semantic Bayesian World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03834)
*Tommaso Soru*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出语义贝叶斯世界模型（SBWMs），旨在解决知识图谱的确定性断言与基础模型的概率推理之间的不匹配问题，将KG从数据管道转变为统一的概率推理架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型与知识图谱的集成仅停留在数据供给层面，缺乏统一的推理架构，原因在于KG的确定性本质与模型的概率推理需求存在根本性错配。

Method: 构建SBWMs框架，利用本体公理约束先验分布，通过贝叶斯条件作用更新信念，并允许行动对世界进行干预；同时规划了实现该框架所需的技术组件，包括RDF信念标注、概率蕴涵机制及校准协议等。

Result: 展示了智能体在复杂场景下的优势：如区分快递员与窃贼、基于蕴而非字符串频率的精算估计、克服LLM弱点的规划任务，以及对未提及数量的估算能力。

Conclusion: 主张社区应致力于开发信念标注标准、概率推理规则和跨代理信念交换协议，以建立动态、共享且可校准的语义贝叶斯世界模型。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs describe reality in crisp assertions, while the systems now consuming them, foundation models and autonomous agents, reason natively in probabilities. We argue that this mismatch is why the integration of language models and knowledge graphs remains a data-feeding pipeline rather than a unified reasoning architecture. We envision Semantic Bayesian World Models (SBWMs): a Web that describes the world not as a database of facts but as a shared, evolving fabric of beliefs over knowledge graphs, where ontological axioms constrain priors, observations update beliefs by Bayesian conditioning, and actions intervene upon the world.
